1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the ensembles space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 773 and the convergence rate is O(503/n^4).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the ensembles space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 773 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 503. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 217.6 (Bound Derivation)

Statement: Let the ensembles structure S satisfy the conditions of bound derivation framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 750. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 797 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 750 is immediate from the bound derivation property. For the inductive step, we decompose the ensembles structure using the bound derivation theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 797. Each subproblem has a solution by the bound derivation fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
